    IMP-3 — history¶

    Item briefs and execution history moved out of PROJECT_PLAN §9 (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

    §9 item 174 — 2026-10-02 (chain I6; queued by the 10:30 review)¶

    Drafted by a read-only Plan agent and reviewed by the 2026-10-02 10:30 daily review. Chain I6 is a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)), and its serial prerequisite GEO-36 landed ✅ in 73f65d05 (item 171). The review amends the §7 Done-when under rule (j) in this commit: W1 ⇒ 🟡, W2 ⇒ ✅. That tightens the row, because the record mesh cannot carry the 1 g ratio. The weekly ratifies.

    IMP-3 step 1 compares the loaded F-small birdcage at 64 MHz with and without a capped PEC rod in the phantom. - Executor: implementer. Tier: heavy, complex: W1 at -n 2, W2 at -n 4. - Cost: ≈ 11 measured / ≈ 55 predicted min. - Depends on: nothing.

    Two findings drive the design: 1. The flag-off record mesh has 537 phantom cells (20261002T110559Z_GEO-36-step2-W2.log:3611). A 6.2 mm 1 g ball therefore holds less than one cell, so the ratio of record needs a second window on the 2.5 mm phantom rung. 2. The phantom absorbs only 3.0e-4 of the source power at 64 MHz. The rod moves the 4×4 below the mesh-noise floor, so the asserted mirror check is a consistency identity. The readings that do separate the rod's mirror are the tip-SAR pairs, and those are printed.

    Fixture and geometry: - Rod. GEO-36's rod: r = 1 mm, ℓ = 30 mm between cap centres (32 mm tip to tip), axis ẑ, centre (0.015, 0, 0), on leg 1's azimuth (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_implant_rod.py:75–79). It is built by _build_rod(**overrides) (:97). - Without the rod: tests/mesh/test_birdcage_port_sheets._build(True, …) (:80). - Entry point. Both meshes enter build_four_port_sweep(frequency_hz=STEP2D_FREQUENCY_HZ, built=<tuple>, build_only=True) (tests/validation/test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:219, keyword doc :234–241, build-only return :516–529). _build_rod() returns exactly the (msh, cell_tags, facet_tags, diag, t) shape it needs. STEP2D_FREQUENCY_HZ = 64e6 (test_birdcage_power_identity.py:182). - Coil. PORT-11 / PORT-16's coil: solid σ = 800 S/m (SIGMA_WIRE_S_PER_M), 50 Ω sheets, untuned at 64 MHz. ANS-7's tuned copper coil is out of reach, because implant_rod refuses as_hole.

    Code: tests only, no src/, no shared-helper edit. - New file tests/validation/test_imp3_rod_birdcage.py, with test_imp3_default_phantom (W1) and test_imp3_one_gram_rung (W2). - Both call a module-level _case(phantom_resolution), which runs these steps in order: 1. Sweep the without-rod arm first. Call run_n_port_sparameter_sweep(problem, port_defs, lumped_sheet_ports=specs, lumped_sheet_facet_tags=facet_tags, keep_fields=True) (pattern at test_port_drive_superposition.py:317–323): one factorisation plus three held solves. 2. Matrix quantities. Z, S, σ(S), column powers and ‖S − Sᵀ‖/‖S‖, by test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:543–557's arithmetic. Classes and spreads come from _circulant_classes (:193) and _class_spread (:176). 3. Power partition per drive. _exact_shares(case, {"driven": pid, "omega": ω, "fields": result.fields[pid]}) (test_birdcage_power_identity.py:313). 4. Quadrature drives. superpose_drives(result, w) (src/fem_em_solver/ports/superposition.py:118), with weights from quadrature_phase_weights(ks, "ccw"/"cw") (test_birdcage_b1_quadrature.py:175) and ks from _port_index (:158). 5. 1 g SAR. mass_averaged_sar(e, sigma=fields.sigma_field, rho=rho_field, center=tip, radius=a_1g, quadrature_degree=QUADRATURE_DEGREE) (src/fem_em_solver/post/sar.py:231). - ρ comes from build_density_field (:147), zero outside tag 3, as in test_birdcage_sar_mass_averaged.py:251–260. - a_1g = averaging_ball_radius(mass_kg=ONE_GRAM_KG, rho=PHANTOM_RHO_KG_PER_M3) = 6.204 mm. - The tips are at (0.015, 0, ±0.016) m. Assert containment: 15 + 6.2 < 30 mm and 16 + 6.2 < 40 mm. 6. Background field, without-rod arm only. Sample E at 9 points on the rod axis, z ∈ [−16, 16] mm, through evaluate_vector_field_parallel on each drive's e_real / e_imag, and combine the superposed drives in numpy. 7. Free the without-rod arm. Store its scalars, then del the result, fields and solver. 8. Rod arm. Build it and repeat steps 1–5. 9. PEC dofs on the rod wall come from the build tuple's facet tags, tag BIRDCAGE_IMPLANT_SURFACE_TAG = 402 (src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:97), via a local three-line copy of _cavity_dofs (test_imp1_pec_sphere_lossy_host.py:191). 10. Closed form. pec_prolate_spheroid_field(p − ROD_CENTRE, semi_major=0.016, semi_minor=0.001, e0=1.0) (src/fem_em_solver/utils/analytical.py:1576), for an equivalent spheroid of aspect 16. - Evaluate it only where the FEM has cells. - Integrate it by a numpy basix degree-8 rule over owned cells, with a ball indicator at the quadrature points, in chunks of 5 000 cells. This is a local _ball_mean_closed_form modelled on test_imp2_spheroid_fem.py:181–201, with χ_ball added. 11. Print before asserting. Every reading is printed before the first assert. W1 also runs the control.

    The mirror symmetry the rod keeps: - σ_y (y → −y), kept. This is the plane through legs 1 and 3 and the axis. On the 4×4 it swaps P2 (90°) ↔ P4 (270°) and fixes P1 (360°) and P3 (180°) (sheet azimuths: 20260912T123236Z_PORT-14-step2d-64mhz.log.gz:1861–1864). - σ_z, kept. The rod is centred at z = 0 and the gaps are centred (io/mesh.py:3337). σ_z maps each port onto itself with its drive reversed, so it does not constrain S. It does force the top and bottom tips to agree. - C4 and σ_x (P1 ↔ P3), broken.

    Anchors (asserted; every band imported): - (A0) Census. All within CELL_COUNT_BAND 0.01 (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_port_sheet_prerequisite.py:57): - W1, without the rod: 116 085 = SOLID_CELL_RECORD (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_conductor_hole.py:95); - W1, with the rod: 167 371 (20261002T110559Z_GEO-36-step2-W2.log:3612); - W2, without the rod: 199 920 = ONE_GRAM_RUNG_CELL_RECORD (test_birdcage_sar_1g_rung.py:125); - W2's rod-arm count is a first record, predicted [225 k, 275 k]. Guard: 320 000, checked before any solve. - (R0) Reproduction, W1 without-rod arm. Z reproduces the 64 MHz record entrywise within REUSE_REPRODUCTION_RTOL 1e-6 (test_port_birdcage_ring_matrix.py:171). - The record is a new module constant, IMP3_FLAG_OFF_Z_64MHZ, taken from …123236Z_PORT-14-step2d-64mhz.log.gz:1866–1869. - It is digit-identical in 20261001T171613Z_PORT-21-step5-W2.log:3670–3673. - (i) Reciprocity, both arms: ‖S − Sᵀ‖/‖S‖ ≤ RECIPROCITY_BAND 1e-3 (test_port_lumped_sheet_sweep.py:86). Without the rod it measured 9.95e-16 (…123236Z…:1875). - (ii) Passivity, both arms: σ_max(S) ≤ 1 + PASSIVITY_SIGMA_TOLERANCE 1e-9 (test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:129), and each column's Σ|S|² ≤ 1. Without the rod: 0.999721388 (:1876). - (P) Power partition, every drive of both arms: P_src,exact = P_vol + P_sheet,exact within DISCRETE_IDENTITY_RTOL 1e-6 (test_birdcage_power_identity.py:227). Measured 3.3e-15 to 7.0e-15 (…123236Z…:1882–1891). - (M) σ_y mirror, rod arm. _class_spread of each of the five pairs (Z₂₂, Z₄₄), (Z₁₂, Z₁₄), (Z₂₁, Z₄₁), (Z₃₂, Z₃₄), (Z₂₃, Z₄₃) is ≤ ADJACENT_SPREAD_BAND 0.005 (test_port_birdcage_lumped_column.py:121). - Without the rod: 0.034 / 0.027 / 0.047 / 0.025 / 0.018 %. - Predicted with the rod: [0.01 %, 0.15 %]. - (E) n × E = 0 on the rod wall, rod arm. pec_facet_tags=None pins every exterior facet (src/fem_em_solver/core/time_harmonic.py:365–367). - The tag-402 dof count, reduced, is > 0. - Max |E| on those dofs is ≤ 1e-12 on every kept drive (IMP-2 (iii)'s bound, test_imp2_spheroid_fem.py:446). - (S) Separation, without-rod arm: the pooled off-diagonal spread is ≥ POOLED_SEPARATION_FLOOR 10× the worst class spread (test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:173). - Asserted, because the same comparison on the same fixture measured 671× (20261001T171613Z_PORT-21-step5-W2.log:3682). - Printed on the rod arm, predicted [300, 1000]×.

    Negative controls (rule (e)): - (C1) The rod wall left natural. W1, P1 only. - Build exterior facets minus 402, tagged OUTER_BOX_TAG 499 (test_th14_birdcage_copper.py:114), with a local copy of _outer_box_tags (:133–157), which subtracts 401 as written. - Assert first that 499 is absent from the mesh, and that the census reads exterior = outer + 402. - Run _solve_driven(dict(case, problem=replace(problem, facet_tags=outer, pec_facet_tags=(OUTER_BOX_TAG,))), "P1") (test_birdcage_b1_plus_map.py:142) on a fresh solver. - Asserted: (P) still closes at 1e-6 (it is a theorem), and the knob is wired: max |E| on the 402 dofs is > 1e-12. - Predicted, printed: the natural-wall tip 1 g over the without-rod tip 1 g in [0.7, 1.3], and PEC over natural in [2, 10]. A ratio of positive SARs has no ceiling, so no factor is claimed. - (C2) What the 4×4 sees of the rod. Predicted, printed. - ΔP_phantom/P_phantom in [1 %, 15 %]. - ΔΣ_i|S_i1|² in [5e-6, 1e-4], and max |ΔS| in [1e-6, 1e-3]. - The σ_x pairs sit at the same mesh floor as the mirror pairs (without the rod: 0.051 %). This 4×4 cannot separate σ_y from C4; that is a scope fact. - (C3) Field-level mirror, both arms and windows. Printed. - The tip 1 g pairs P2 / P4, ccw / cw and top / bottom: each predicted ≤ 1 %. C4_COVARIANCE_BAND 5 % is shown as a reference, never asserted. - P1 / P3, related only by the broken σ_x: predicted [50 %, 95 %], ceiling 100 %.

    Printed beside predictions written first. None of these is asserted; their accuracy is ANS-10's. - The ratio of record (W2; W1's coarse value beside it): Q = SAR_1g,tip(rod) / SAR_1g,tip(without) at both tips, for P1–P4, ccw and cw. Predicted [2, 10]. - Ball masses: 1.000 g without the rod; predicted 0.9817 g with it. - The closed form: - M_cf = ⟨|E_cf|²⟩ / E₀² on the same quadrature-masked region, predicted [2.5, 6]; - L_z(aspect 16) = 0.009693 (prolate_spheroid_depolarisation, analytical.py:1493); - the axial fraction η_z, predicted [0.5, 1.0]; - the estimate η_z·M_cf + (1 − η_z), with Q / estimate predicted in [0.6, 2.5]. - The quasi-static scale: (|k|·c_eq)² ≈ 7.4 % (|k| = 17.0 1/m, from SALINE_SIGMA 0.5 and SALINE_EPSILON_R 78). - Also: P_phantom per drive, the narrowed facet counts per sheet, ΔS, cells, times and the summed peak RSS.

    Windows.     Traps already paid for: - Build order. Build without the rod first, then with it, in both windows: a failed gmsh build contaminates the process (GEO-23). The rod + 0.0025 combination has never been built; a FAIL is recorded verbatim and never retried. - built= ignores mesh-sizing keywords. Pass phantom_resolution=PHANTOM_RESOLUTION_1G_RUNG to _build / _build_rod themselves. - Tag 402 lives in the build tuple, not in the sweep's facet tags. Keep that handle. - A held factorisation silently solves a different problem (PORT-19, WF-12). The control uses _solve_driven's fresh solver. - Point evaluation inside the hole raises (post/sar.py:357–361). The axis samples therefore come from the without-rod arm only. - Rank-local quantities. assemble_scalar, cell_tags.values and locate_dofs_topological are rank-local: reduce them, and count owned dofs only. Keep collectives outside any rank == 0 branch. - No UFL comparisons, and no sqrt comparisons. Do the ball mask and the closed form in numpy. - Free the without-rod arm before the rod build in W2. - Harness discipline: - always -s; node IDs only; no pytest -k; no pipes; - Status 124 is the finding: pgrep -c python3, then the cache sweep, and never lengthen the timeout; - no module edit after W1 starts (rule (i)).

    Scope: degree 1, 64 MHz, the untuned solid-coil 4×4 with 50 Ω sheets, and one rod on leg 1's azimuth, axial. Not covered: - the accuracy of the tip ratio (ANS-10); - absolute or pointwise SAR, heating, compliance or convergence; - the tuned or copper coil; - F-human; - other rod placements; - ISO 10974 (WF-9) and ASTM F2182 (7c).

    Aspect 16 lies beyond IMP-2's FEM-checked aspects (2 and 5).

    §9 item 235 — 2026-10-04 (chain I9)¶

    Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-04 18:00 daily review; the review reviews it. Licence: §10 chain I9 (PROJECT_PLAN.md ≈ line 4598), a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)); ANS-10 was re-homed to this Palace twin by the 10-03 weekly (≈ line 4483). No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves.

    IMP-3 step 3 — the Palace twin. Palace's 4×4 at 64 MHz, order 1, on the rod fixture with and without the rod, its own identities asserted; ours at degree 1 on the same exported meshes; ΔS(rod) per class, ours against Palace, printed. - Executor: implementer. New probe + new test module; no src/, no edit to any existing file. - Tier: heavy. Export -n 1; Palace -np 8; ours -n 2. Complex container for every non-Palace window. - Class: chain step (solve), measured median ≈ 18–23 min. - Cost: ≈ 20 measured / ≈ 45 predicted min (15 fixed + ≈ 15 code + ≈ 7 compute + ≈ 8 spare). - Depends on: nothing. GEO-36's hole build and leg_sheet_strip_fraction are both on main.

    Settled from the code (read before designing). - The rod is a PEC hole, not a meshed region. _build_rod() (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_implant_rod.py:125) cuts the rod out of the phantom; its wall is exterior facet tag BIRDCAGE_IMPLANT_SURFACE_TAG = 402, every 402 facet exterior with its cell in tag 3 (GEO-36 (A5)). Ours pins it by the default exterior PEC (pec_facet_tags=None, IMP-3 (E), 7 437 dofs, max |E| 0). Palace therefore needs the rod wall as its own boundary attribute listed under PEC. The kept-cells build (keep_cells=True, item 219) is WF-9's, not this item's. - IMP-3's fixture uses ragged port sheets. test_birdcage_port_sheets._build(True) / _build_rod() pass no leg_sheet_strip_fraction, so build_four_port_sweep narrows each sheet by the midpoint filter: 26 triangles per port (20261002T170428Z_IMP-3-step1-W1.log:1837,3646). Palace refuses that set (MFEM abort: Specified direction does not align …, 20260929T110504Z_OPS-61-step1a-W3.log:86), and the 10-02 binding ruling makes a new fixture's ports exact CAD rectangles. So this twin runs on the exact-rectangle sibling of IMP-3's fixture: the same two builders with leg_sheet_strip_fraction=GATED_WIDTH_FRACTION. Nothing in the generator refuses the rod + strip combination (io/mesh.py:4244–4271), but it has never been built. IMP-3's ragged records stay valid as records. - Ours' ΔS(rod) per class at degree 1 is not a tracked record. IMP-3 W1 printed both arms' S to 10 digits (…170428Z_IMP-3-step1-W1.log:3682–3685, 3704–3707) and max |ΔS| = 2.663e-4 (:3727; W2 1.32e-4). No per-class ΔS and no module constant exist, and those are ragged meshes anyway. Computed from those lines for scale (this brief's arithmetic, not a record): |Δ mean_k| = 1.25e-4 / 3.48e-5 / 3.80e-5, i.e. 2.1e-4 / 8.3e-5 / 1.1e-4 of |mean_k| (self / adjacent / opposite). The same arms' class spreads are 0.057 / 0.060 / 0.037 % without the rod (:3687). ΔS(rod) between two separately meshed arms is therefore at or below the remesh floor. Ours against Palace on the same mesh pair is still a sharp test, because both codes see the same two meshes. But the remesh-free rod effect is ΔS_wall = S(rod mesh, PEC wall) − S(rod mesh, natural wall), on one mesh. That is printed beside ΔS(rod).

    Printed, never gated (ours against Palace, P1 the reading of record, P2–P4 printed beside): - (B) The owned-cell count per point, predicted 1 everywhere. A point reading ≠ 1 sits on a face, where the two codes may evaluate different cells. It is flagged face-ambiguous and left out of (T_E). This is a filter, not a stop. - (T_E) The field tie. g = E^ours_c(F1) / E^Pal_c(F1) on the without-rod arm, with c* = argmax_c |E^Pal_c(F1)|. - Per arm, the reading is max over (p, c) of |E^ours_c(p) − g·E^Pal_c(p)| / ‖g·E^Pal(p)‖ over the five non-reference points plus F1's other components. Predicted ≤ 1e-6 on all three arms. - Derivation: same mesh, same N1curl order 1 and the same discrete operator (S tied at ≤ 2.2e-10, item 235). Evaluation is in the same cell, so the residual is solver tolerance (Palace Tol 1e-10) times a local amplification. The 1e4 margin covers that amplification. - Print g itself (the ratio of incident scales; a constant, no prediction), and the same reading against conj(E^Pal): predicted O(1), which confirms the convention. - (T_W) The energy tie. q_a = W^ours_ph,a / W^Pal_ph,a for P1. The reading is max_a |q_a / q_off − 1|, predicted ≤ 1e-6. Print q_off / |g|² (the codes' energy-prefactor convention; a constant, no prediction). - The rod's tip-field effect, inside each code, then ours against Palace: - η_wall(p) = |E_rodPEC(p)| / |E_rodnat(p)|: one mesh, no remesh. Predicted for P1: - T1 [1.5, 20], T2 [1.2, 8], T3 [1.05, 4]; - S1 [0.2, 1.5] (n × E = 0 suppresses the axial field beside the shaft); - F1, F2 |η − 1| ∈ [3e-3, 1e-1]. This is a PEC-spheroid dipole estimate: a·b²/(3 L_z) = 5.5e-7 m³ over r³ = 2.7e-5 / 1.7e-5 m³ ≈ 2 % / 3 %, with L_z(16) = 0.009692 (item 174 W2). - η_rod(p) = |E_rod(p)| / |E_off(p)|: two meshes, the remesh included. The tip predictions are as for η_wall. At F1 and F2, |η − 1| ∈ [1e-3, 0.3]; the without-rod phantom has 537 cells, so a point value moves with the remesh. - Ours against Palace: d_η = |η^ours − η^Pal|, predicted ≤ 2e-6·η, i.e. two ratio errors at (T_E)'s level. - ΔW: W_ph(rod) / W_ph(rodnat) − 1 and W_ph(rod) / W_ph(off) − 1, per code and per drive, ours against Palace. Predicted |Δ| ∈ [1e-3, 0.1] and code gap ≤ 1e-6. The rod's tip effect exceeds the codes' disagreement by 5.2e8×. This is not a 1 g SAR, h- or p-statement.

    Scope. Covered: Palace order 2 and ours at degree 2, 64 MHz, on item 235's exact-rectangle record meshes (117 206 / 168 507 cells, σ = 800 S/m untuned coil, 50 Ω, κ off), on two arms (without the rod, and rod PEC). The comparison is E at six fixed points and phantom electric energy, P1 of record. The order 1 → 2 shift of the near-tip field is printed per code. Agreement is a matched-order check at the production order between two independent codes. The order shift is a one-mesh p-reading, not an h-statement and not a convergence rate (two orders, one mesh). Not covered: - the 1 g tip SAR, the ratio Q and its accuracy.     Question. What does the rod's own SAR do to the F-small gel phantom's temperature, compared with the same coil drive without the rod? This is the first implant-driven heating on a coil-driven phantom.

    Review ruling 2026-10-25 18:00 (item 639, I41) — audited PASS; the re-set reproduction bound accepted, with its reading recorded¶

    The auditor returned PASS. Evidence re-cited: - (A0m) exact before any solve: test_imp3_rod_heating_matched.py:147–149, log 20261025T201613Z_IMP-3-I41-W1b.log:3645. - (B) / (E) / (E′) / (S2) on both arms at the imported B_BAND (test_mat10_sar_to_heat_sphere.py:61) and E_BAND / S2_BAND (test_mat12_transient_sar_heating_sphere.py:75–76): source :253–256, log :3680–3683 and :3725–3728. - (C4) on arm M at C4_COVARIANCE_BAND (test_birdcage_b1_plus_map.py:120): source :263, log :3686–3689. - 16 passed (:3791); Status 0, 290 s (:3797–3798).

    The W1 red and the re-set. W1 failed on |S2 − I39_ROD_S2| ≤ 5e-12, reading 2.969e-11 (20261025T201043Z_IMP-3-I41-W1.log:3794–3797). That bound was the executor's own, in a new and unlanded module. The brief asked only for reproduction "to the printed digits' order". I39 prints |ratio − 1| as 4.9999e-06, five significant digits, so 1e-10 is that order. The relaxation is not a loosening in the CLAUDE.md sense: no imported band, no §4 anchor and no brief-named gate moved, and the measurement is recorded in the code (:270–274). Arm M's own (S2) ratio also differs in the 12th digit between W1 and W1b on identical inputs, so a floor near 1e-12 exists anyway. Two caveats are recorded, and neither changes the result: - Arm R is not literally I39's arm. Its normalisation G is arm M's (+0.263 %, W1b.log:3723 against I39-W1.log:3661), so only P and the mesh are I39's. - The brief's "(B)–(S2) reproduce I39" control holds only for P_phantom and (S2). (B), (E) and (E′) are held to their bands, not to I39's values.

    Standing reading for future briefs: a reproduction control names its numeric bound in the brief, or names the print whose last digit sets it. An executor that tightens beyond that and then relaxes after a red writes both values and the measurement, as here. I41 stays provisional until the 10-26 weekly ratifies chain I's step.
